<h1>Your Etsy Privacy Settings</h1>
<p>In order to give you the best experience, we use cookies and similar technologies for performance, analytics, personalisation, advertising, and to help our site function. Want to know more? Read our <a href="/uk/legal/cookies-and-tracking-technologies">Cookie Policy</a>. You can change your preferences any time in your <a href="/uk/your/account/privacy">Privacy Settings</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="display-flex-sm justify-content-flex-end align-items-baseline">
<div class="text-center">
<button class="width-full btn btn-transparent text-gray-darker mb-xs-2 mb-sm-0" data-gdpr-open-full-settings="" type="button">Update settings</button>
</div>
<div class="">
<button class="width-full btn btn-outline btn-outline-black" data-gdpr-single-choice-accept="" type="button">Accept</button>
</div>

<div aria-hidden="true" class="ui-toolkit" data-aria-hidden-by-contain-focus="true">
<div aria-hidden="true" aria-labelledby="gdpr-full-settings-overlay-title" class="overlay-region display-none" data-toolkit-overlay="" id="gdpr-privacy-settings" role="dialog" style="z-index: 1001;">
<div class="overlay-view gdpr-overlay-view">
<div class="overlay-header gdpr-overlay-header">
<span class="overlay-title" id="gdpr-full-settings-overlay-title">Privacy Settings</span>
</div>
<div class="overlay-body gdpr-overlay-body">
<div class="privacySettings">
<div class="privacySettings__section privacySettings__section--first" data-section="intro">
<p>Etsy uses cookies and similar technologies to give you a better experience, enabling things like:</p>
<ul><li>basic site functions</li>
<li>ensuring secure, safe transactions</li>
<li>secure account login</li>
<li>remembering account, browser, and regional preferences</li>
<li>remembering privacy and security settings</li>
<li>analysing site traffic and usage</li>
<li>personalised search, content, and recommendations</li>
<li>helping sellers understand their audience</li>
<li>showing relevant, targeted ads on and off Etsy</li>
</ul><p>Detailed information can be found in Etsy’s <a href="/uk/legal/cookies-and-tracking-technologies">Cookies &amp; Similar Technologies Policy</a> and our <a href="/uk/legal/privacy">Privacy Policy</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="privacySettings__section" data-section="cookies">
<div class="privacySettings__toggleLabel">Always on</div>
<h2>Required Cookies &amp; Technologies</h2>
<p>Some of the technologies we use are necessary for critical functions like security and site integrity, account authentication, security and privacy preferences, internal site usage and maintenance data, and to make the site work correctly for browsing and transactions.</p>
</div>
<div class="privacySettings__section" data-section="personalization_consent">
<label aria-hidden="true" class="privacySettings__toggleLabel" data-gdpr-toggle-label="" for="personalization_consent">
                Off
        </label>
<div class="privacySettings__toggle">
<input class="switch-checkbox" data-checked-label="On" data-gdpr-toggle="" data-unchecked-label="Off" id="personalization_consent" name="personalization_consent" type="checkbox"/>
<label aria-hidden="true" class="switch" for="personalization_consent"></label>
</div>
<h2>Site Customisation</h2>
<p>Cookies and similar technologies are used to improve your experience, to do things like:</p>
<ul><li>remember your login, general, and regional preferences</li>
<li>personalise content, search, recommendations, and offers</li>
</ul><p>Without these technologies, things like personalised recommendations, your account preferences, or localisation may not work correctly. Find out more in our <a href="/uk/legal/cookies-and-tracking-technologies">Cookies &amp; Similar Technologies Policy</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="privacySettings__section" data-section="third_party_consent">
<label aria-hidden="true" class="privacySettings__toggleLabel" data-gdpr-toggle-label="" for="third_party_consent">
                Off
        </label>
<div class="privacySettings__toggle">
<input class="switch-checkbox" data-checked-label="On" data-gdpr-toggle="" data-unchecked-label="Off" id="third_party_consent" name="third_party_consent" type="checkbox"/>
<label aria-hidden="true" class="switch" for="third_party_consent"></label>
</div>
<h2>Personalised Advertising</h2>
<p>These technologies are used for things like:</p>
<ul><li>personalised ads</li>
<li>to limit how many times you see an ad</li>
<li>to understand usage via Google Analytics</li>
<li>to understand how you got to Etsy</li>
<li>to ensure that sellers understand their audience and can provide relevant ads</li>
</ul><p>We do this with social media, marketing, and analytics partners (who may have their own information they’ve collected). Saying no will not stop you from seeing Etsy ads, but it may make them less relevant or more repetitive. Find out more in our <a href="/uk/legal/cookies-and-tracking-technologies">Cookies &amp; Similar Technologies Policy</a>.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="text-center body-max-width pt-xs-3 pb-xs-5 pt-xl-6 pb-xl-6">
<h2 class="wt-text-heading-02 mb-xs-2"> What is Etsy? </h2>
<a class="wt-text-link" href="/uk/about"> Read our wonderfully weird story </a>
<div class="col-group pt-xs-6">
<div class="col-md-10 col-offset-md-1 col-lg-8 col-offset-lg-2 col-offset-xl-0 col-xl-4 mb-xs-4 mb-lg-6 mb-xl-2 pr-sm-4 pl-sm-4 pr-xs-2 pl-xs-2">
<h3 class="wt-text-heading-01 mb-xs-1"> A one-of-a-kind community </h3>
<p class="wt-text-body-01"> Etsy is a global online marketplace, where people come together to make, sell, buy and collect unique items. </p>
</div>
<div class="col-md-10 col-offset-md-1 col-lg-8 col-offset-lg-2 col-offset-xl-0 col-xl-4 mb-xs-4 mb-lg-6 mb-xl-2 pr-sm-4 pl-sm-4 pr-xs-2 pl-xs-2">
<h3 class="wt-text-heading-01 mb-xs-1"> Support independent creators </h3>
<p class="wt-text-body-01"> There’s no Etsy warehouse – just millions of people selling the things they love. We make the whole process easy, helping you connect directly with makers to find something extraordinary. </p>
</div>
<div class="col-md-10 col-offset-md-1 col-lg-8 col-offset-lg-2 col-offset-xl-0 col-xl-4 mb-xs-4 mb-lg-6 mb-xl-2 pr-sm-4 pl-sm-4 pr-xs-2 pl-xs-2">
<h3 class="wt-text-heading-01 mb-xs-1"> Peace of mind </h3>
<p class="wt-text-body-01"> Your privacy is the highest priority of our dedicated team. And if you ever need assistance, we are always ready to step in for support. </p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-group">
<h4 class="wt-text-title-02 mt-xs-1 mt-xl-4"> Have a question? Well, we’ve got some answers. </h4>
<a class="wt-mt-xs-2 button wt-btn wt-text-black wt-btn--outline" href="/uk/help">Go to Help Center</a>
</div>
